Proof 1: Seismic wave velocities: P-waves faster than S-waves

Statement: Seismic wave velocities: P-waves faster than S-waves

Hypotheses: elastic medium, earthquake source

Conclusion: Vp > Vs always

Definitions: P-wave, S-wave, seismic

Proof (Variation #1):

Step 1: P-wave speed: Vp = sqrt((K + 4/3 mu)/rho)

Step 2: S-wave speed: Vs = sqrt(mu/rho)

Step 3: K > 0 (bulk modulus), so Vp > Vs

Step 4: S-waves cannot travel through liquids (mu=0)

ID: dd296799c7c9 | Status: UNFORMALIZED | Complexity: O(n)

Mutation test: This is a physical law derived from elasticity theory

Provenance: Variation #1 for theorem in domain Earth_Sciences

Hypotheses: elastic medium, earthquake source

Conclusion: Vp > Vs always

Definitions: P-wave, S-wave, seismic

Proof (Variation #373):

Step 1: P-wave speed: Vp = sqrt((K + 4/3 mu)/rho)

Step 2: S-wave speed: Vs = sqrt(mu/rho)

Step 3: K > 0 (bulk modulus), so Vp > Vs

Step 4: S-waves cannot travel through liquids (mu=0)

Proof 485: Seismic wave velocities: P-waves faster than S-waves

Hypotheses: elastic medium, earthquake source

Conclusion: Vp > Vs always

Definitions: P-wave, S-wave, seismic

Step 2: S-wave speed: Vs = sqrt(mu/rho)

Step 4: S-waves cannot travel through liquids (mu=0)

Proof 708: Seismic wave velocities: P-waves faster than S-waves

Statement: Seismic wave velocities: P-waves faster than S-waves

Hypotheses: elastic medium, earthquake source

Conclusion: Vp > Vs always

Definitions: P-wave, S-wave, seismic

Step 1: P-wave speed: Vp = sqrt((K + 4/3 mu)/rho)

Step 2: S-wave speed: Vs = sqrt(mu/rho)

Step 3: K > 0 (bulk modulus), so Vp > Vs

Step 4: S-waves cannot travel through liquids (mu=0)

Proof 782: Seismic wave velocities: P-waves faster than S-waves

Statement: Seismic wave velocities: P-waves faster than S-waves

Hypotheses: elastic medium, earthquake source

Conclusion: Vp > Vs always

Definitions: P-wave, S-wave, seismic

Step 2: S-wave speed: Vs = sqrt(mu/rho)

Step 3: K > 0 (bulk modulus), so Vp > Vs

Step 4: S-waves cannot travel through liquids (mu=0)

Provenance: Variation #818 for theorem in domain Earth_Sciences

Proof 819: Seismic wave velocities: P-waves faster than S-waves

Statement: Seismic wave velocities: P-waves faster than S-waves

Hypotheses: elastic medium, earthquake source

Conclusion: Vp > Vs always

Definitions: P-wave, S-wave, seismic

Proof (Variation #819):

Step 1: P-wave speed: Vp = sqrt((K + 4/3 mu)/rho)

Step 2: S-wave speed: Vs = sqrt(mu/rho)

Step 3: K > 0 (bulk modulus), so Vp > Vs

Step 4: S-waves cannot travel through liquids (mu=0)
